## `AGO-5912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** DCP-Checksum-KDM Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Dolby Vision HDR & Dolby Atmos Multi-Track Specification QC
- **Intro:** DCP-Checksum-KDM Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Dolby Vision HDR & Dolby Atmos Multi-Track Specification QC — Verify Steady-State Environment: Room temperature: 21.0°C ± 0.5°C. Relative humidity: 45% ± 5% RH. Mains voltage: 230 VAC ± 2% (225.4–234.6 VAC). Frequency: 50 Hz ± 0.5 Hz.
- **Key tolerance:** Room temperature: 21.0°C ± 0.5°C. Relative humidity: 45% ± 5% RH. Mains voltage: 230 VAC ± 2% (225.4–234.6 VAC). Frequency: 50 Hz ± 0.5 Hz.
- **Standards:** `ISO/IEC 14496-12:2022`, `ST 429-2:2021`, `ST 429-6:2021`, `ST 429-10:2021`
